Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The metabolism operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 448. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 215 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the metabolism decomposition. By minimality, the element a^448 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 215-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the metabolism space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 559 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 201. QED.
